Blinding flames erupted in midair as debris scattered, as though dozens of fireworks had been set off.

I have to say that a Pyromaniac's powers are very useful at sea. It's a mystic version of a ballistic missile defense system. However, Anderson is the Strongest Hunter and has already reached Sequence 5. If it were Danitz, there'd be no way he could block them all. It would be impressive if he could detonate even half... Sigh, when I use Flame Controlling, I can only deal with one cannonball at a time... As Klein sighed inwardly, the Black Tulip ceased its bombardment, changed speed again, and closed the gap with the Future.

As it approached, the surface of the sea between the two gigantic sailboats which was dyed with golden sunlight rapidly turned black. It didn't look like ink, but the darkest night without any moon or stars.

Translucent and illusory ferocious creatures crawled out of this dark sea. Pale corpses reached out their palms which had rotten flesh dangling from them as dark red or cold flames emerged, forming pairs of eyes.

Suddenly, the area seemed to turn into the entrance to hell. All kinds of undead creatures floated on the surface of the sea in dense numbers as they charged forward like an unending tidal wave.

Anderson looked back at the Sun Wraiths which were obliterating the grayish-black flesh, and he knew that Admiral of Stars was at a critical moment in dealing with the terrifying monster. All he could do was draw a gasp and look up at Gehrman Sparrow with a bitter smile.

Just as he was about to press his hands against the ship's side and create scarlet flames to halt the invading undead army, he was surprised to see Gehrman Sparrow turn around and fly back into his cabin.

H-he fled? No way... Anderson's expression clearly froze.

He grimaced as he hurriedly leaned forward with his extended palms to press onto the shipboard.

Silently, a swath of scarlet flames soared into the sky and swept the area ahead of him.

At this moment, a charm suddenly fell in front of him as he heard a word spoken in ancient Hermes.

"Storm!"

Huh? Anderson instinctively looked up and saw Gehrman Sparrow, in his round-necked shirt and brown jacket, swept up by a squall as he rapidly flew toward the Black Tulip.

I-is he committing suicide? I can't understand such madness... Anderson stared agape, utterly baffled.

Whoosh!

With the charm's effects and the wind's blessing, Klein flew into the airspace above the Black Tulip's bow.

Admiral Hell Ludwell immediately looked up at him. The eyes behind the silver mask burned silently with two pale-white flames.

The shadows, wraiths, and the spirit world creatures of the Death domain who were spiraling above the Black Tulip immediately swarmed towards the enemy.

Some opened their mouths to unleash silent screeches; others extended long tongues with shrunken faces at their tips, as though overcome with excitement.

Klein looked at them with a deadpan expression as he reached into his pocket with his left hand, releasing the wall of spirituality on a squarish box.

Then, he took out Azik's copper whistle, which was tied to a few matchsticks, and threw them at the deck of the Black Tulip's stern.

Suddenly, the transparent, cold, and indistinct shadows and wraiths paused. It was as though they were frozen worms.

Without hesitation, they turned and flew rapidly toward the stern of the Black Tulip. The strange-looking spirit world creatures followed close behind.

In less than a second, the skeletons and zombies who were adjusting the cannons betrayed Admiral Hell. The bow was left completely empty except for Ludwell.

Pa!

Pressing down on his cap, Klein landed on the deck directly in front of Ludwell.

His body was bent slightly as he released the right hand on his cap, his gaze locked on the silver-masked Admiral Hell.

Chapter 659: This Beyonder Power Is Very Powerful

On the Future, Anderson Hood pressed his hands on the shipboard and saw the undead army in the pitch-black sea recede like the tide. They rushed over one another and surged for the stern of the Black Tulip. He saw Gehrman Sparrow press his cap with his right hand while he descended from the sky before landing opposite Admiral Hell Ludwell.

The scene was illuminated by dark-red and gloomy-green light. Framed by wraiths, shadows, and all sorts of strange spirit world creatures, it possessed an indescribable beauty.

Cool! As expected of the craziest adventurer... Anderson marveled inwardly before recalling something.

Gehrman Sparrow had apparently thrown a charm in front of him before flying over. Furthermore, he had deliberately demonstrated the incantation that activated it!

His intent is... Anderson Hood moved his gaze down and discovered a charm made of tin at his feet.

On the Black Tulip, Klein, whose body was slightly bent while his eyes were locked onto his enemy, wasn't actually as cold and calm as his expression depicted.

Anderson had better fly over with that charm. There's a good chance I can't deal with him alone. It might even be extremely dangerous... With the silver mask and its two pale-white flames reflected in his eyes, Klein prayed silently.

Because of the mysterious eyes that had observed him and the deck, as well as the person Anderson Hood had mentioned opening a door in the dream world, he cautiously abandoned the idea of immediately praying to himself and responding with the Sea God Scepter from above the gray fog. He warned himself not to expose such matters unless he was in dire straits.

He believed that Creeping Hunger and Tinder, together with the various Beyonder powers of Magician and the charms of the Sea God domain, gave him the ability to fight Admiral Hell Ludwell. As for Azik's copper whistle, it could attract undead creatures or spirit world creatures inclined toward the Death domain, neutralizing a powerful Spirit Medium's most potent means. A Sequence 5 Beyonder of the Death pathway would often choose to overwhelm a group of Mid-Sequence Beyonders with sheer numbers, provided the group wasn't too large.

However, Klein didn't believe that he alone could defeat Ludwell in such a situation, much less kill him. First, the battlefield was the Black Tulip. Considering how Admiral of Stars could use the Future, it was obvious that fighting Ludwell aboard his own ship was far from ideal. Second, Ludwell was currently the most senior pirate admiral. He had the backing of two powerful factions, the King of the Five Seas and the Numinous Episcopate. His mystical items and Sealed Artifacts might be no weaker than Klein's and could even surpass them. In addition, many rumors indicated that he possessed a ring left behind by the ancient Death!

With his Sequence lower than Admiral Hell's, Klein felt none of the exhilaration of a hunt nearing success or the thrill of masterfully acting as a crazy adventurer. Instead, he was tense and anxious and didn't dare be careless. He only hoped that the Strongest Hunter plagued by bad luck would fly over quickly.

Only by joining forces with someone of comparable strength would he have even a small chance of defeating or resisting Ludwell, who had lost his undead army. It would also give Cattleya and her pirates the time and space to finish off the composite monster.

Just as this thought flashed across his mind, Klein didn't hesitate to launch an attack. He made the Fog of Poison which he stole with Tinder spread.

No one could see the uneasiness and worry in his heart.

Wearing an exaggerated triangular hat and silver mask, Ludwell raised his clenched left fist, spread his fingers, and aimed his palm at Klein.

The bow's deck was instantly enveloped in a harrowing yellowish-green fog. Before Ludwell, an illusory glow exploded. Then a point spiraled and collapsed inward, outlining a blurry bronze door whose two halves swung open.

The bronze door was covered in all sorts of mysterious patterns. It had an indescribable heaviness and silence to it.

With a creak, the door shook and cracked open a little.

Behind the gap was endless darkness, as though it was the deepest and darkest night.

Indescribable pairs of eyes were hidden in the darkness behind the door. They were densely packed and everywhere, but it was impossible to discern their actual bodies.

Skinless arms covered in blood and greenish-black vines bearing baby faces reached out. Palms with tooth-filled mouths grabbed at everything beyond the door as they screamed, laughed, sobbed, and yelled.

This brought a terrifying suction force. Out of nowhere, cold hurricanes that sent chills down the bone stirred, pushing objects towards these strange creatures and the gap in the bronze door!

The yellowish-green Fog of Poison was instantly cleared as Klein couldn't help but lean forward as he stumbled.

The glove on his left palm immediately turned pitch-black. It had the sinister feel of the night and the grandeur of the cosmos.

Klein's brown eyes darkened as he spread out his left arm to the side in a gesture of politeness.

The terrifying suction force that swept the bow's deck suddenly changed direction, "grabbing" the skeletons and rotting zombies that were rushing to the stern, and it threw them into the gap of the door. They were bound by the greenish-black, baby-faced vines and bloody arms and were pulled behind the bronze door where the countless eyes were.

"Distortion!"

Baron of Corruption's Distortion!

Klein had distorted the target of the mysterious door, and he used the skeletons and zombies on the Black Tulip to substitute himself.

Despite that, he was still affected by the remnant forces of the tremendous suction force. He found it difficult to take a step, preventing him from fully making use of his nimble and agile traits.

The hurricane had already swept his cap into the air, where it spun as though taking flight in pursuit of the undead creatures being sucked away.

At this moment, Admiral Hell Ludwell, who wore his exaggerated triangular hat, raised his right hand again and extended his palm.

The right side of his upper body rapidly turned illusory, as though it belonged to a specter or wraith. His arm extended continuously, instantly crossing a considerable distance as his pale palm grabbed at his enemy.

Whoosh!

The hurricane's roar suddenly vanished as soft sobs pierced Klein's ears, numbing his body as though his blood had frozen.

As the pale palm approached, he seemed to be possessed by a wraith or evil spirit. He couldn't produce an effective response as he watched death approach. In despair, he felt his vitality deplete at an increasing rate.

Without any resistance, Ludwell's pale and illusory right palm grabbed Klein and squashed him into a thin paper figurine.

The paper figurine was covered in signs of dark-green corrosion. Soon, the unceasing hurricane shredded it to dust.

To the side of the bronze door, Klein's figure surfaced again. His left glove had already been dyed with the color of pure sunlight.

He immediately straightened his body and spread open his arms.

Golden flames swirled around a pillar of holy light that descended from the sky, striking the bronze door which was covered in mysterious patterns.

Sunlight suddenly burst out, and it was so blinding that it made it almost impossible for Klein to open his eyes. As for the terrifying door which Ludwell had created, it had begun shaking and turning slightly blurry. Even the extraordinary suction force from behind the door had weakened. More than half the greenish-black baby-faced vines and the bloody arms were vaporized.

However, even more strange arms and distorted creatures attempted to squeeze out from the opening in the door.

Just as Klein was about to continue using the Priest of Light's Light of Purification to purify the bronze door, Ludwell's pale palm swiped down wildly.

Klein hurriedly dodged to the side as he kept rolling to avoid the remnant effects of the hurricane and the soul-sucking palm.

One roll, two rolls, three rolls, and his body abruptly sprang up at an angle. At some point, Creeping Hunger had taken on the appearance of gold.

Admiral Hell's silver mask was the first thing that was reflected in Klein's eyes. It included the pale flames in his eye sockets. Following that, two bolts of lightning brightened from the depths of his eyes.

Interrogator's Psychic Piercing!

At this moment, a black, square-shaped ring on Ludwell's left index finger produced a slight glow.

Immediately a scene appeared in Klein's mind.

It was a gigantic throne made from the rotting heads of humans, elves, giants, dragons, demonic wolves, sea monsters, vampires, and other creatures. On either side were miniature, transparent faces of wraiths, shadows, and evil spirits, all filled with hatred, viciousness, and indignation.

Suddenly, Klein felt as though his head had been struck by an axe. The fearsome pain filled his mind without any delay.

His Psychic Piercing had not only failed to show its effects, but it had even ended up affecting him in an amplified manner!

If not for him having experienced more extreme pain, Klein definitely would've fallen to the ground, wailing and struggling. But even so, he temporarily lost his ability to resist as he bent his back with a grimace.

Seizing this opportunity, Ludwell uttered a slow language through the opening over his mouth, one ordinary living creatures couldn't understand. The surroundings instantly turned dark, blurry, and illusory.

This was the Language of Death that came from hell and the Underworld!

Just as Klein felt a little better, he discovered that his Spirit Body was floating up uncontrollably, separating from his body, inch by inch!

And the terrifying suction force from the bronze door was an irresistible force to the spirit.

No, this won't do! While his Spirit Body hadn't completely left his body, Klein raised his right arm and spread his hand which was wearing Tinder with great difficulty.

Different lustrous glows instantly interwove as they surfaced before him, constantly changing and flickering rapidly.

Without any hesitation, Klein grabbed onto a pale-white blob swirling with a gloomy green color. He twisted his wrist and extracted it.

In this battle, the Beyonder power he wished to steal the most was the one that created the bronze door, but he couldn't guarantee his success. All he could do was seek the blessings of the Goddess.

The lustrous glow flew over and landed in Tinder.

This wasn't the Beyonder power Klein wanted most, but it wasn't the worst result either.

The mouth behind Ludwell's silver mask moved, but it couldn't produce the sluggish, awkward language incomprehensible to the living.

At the same time, Klein opened his mouth.

Chapter 660: Behind the Mask

One distorted, incomprehensible, jarring syllable after another slowly spilled from Klein's mouth. The hurricane created by the bronze door's terrifying suction instantly subsided, cloaking the already dim surroundings in deeper gloom.

Only then did he learn that the Beyonder power Admiral Hell Ludwell had just used was known as the Language of the Dead. It could bypass the protection of flesh and blood to target the Spirit Body directly.

It was an advanced form of a Spirit Medium's powers, progressing beyond direct communication with spirits to commandeering and even enslaving them!

Living creatures couldn't understand the resounding language. Ludwell froze in place as a transparent layer rapidly surfaced over his pirate captain's attire.

His spirit was being tugged at by an illusory power!

At this moment, the black, square-shaped ring on Ludwell's left index finger produced a faint glow.

The tiny portion of his Spirit Body that had been forcefully drawn out returned, fusing with his body once more.

Ding!

With his right hand, Ludwell drew the thin rapier hanging by his waist.

It was iron-black in color, and its tip gathered the surrounding light, turning into a dark point.

Admiral Hell suddenly stepped forward. Accompanied by violent currents of air, he instantly closed the distance to Klein and thrust his rapier at lightning speed!

The bronze door covered in mysterious patterns remained standing in its original position. It didn't vanish when Ludwell retracted his left hand and took further action. This differed from the similar-looking ability Miss Sharron had previously used through a mystical item.

Oof!

The black rapier pierced through Klein in an unavoidable manner.

Klein's figure rapidly crumpled into a piece of paper. Its surface turned yellow and dry, as though it had been weathered for thousands of years.

The hurricane brought about by the bronze door completely pulverized the paper figurine.

In midair, Klein jumped out of the darkness as he held a huge handful of charms from the Sea God domain.

"Storm!"

He quickly shouted in ancient Hermes. The tin flakes lit up one by one as they sacrificed themselves to Sea God. This also meant that, if Klein wished, he could recycle most of the material and use it repeatedly until the metal could no longer withstand the spirituality.

Whoosh!

Blue wind blades shot out as the surrounding waters surged into massive waves as tall as the ship. Because Klein neither attempted nor had time to distinguish between the different kinds of charms, their extraordinary effects also augmented Admiral Hell even as their attacks inundated him. Ludwell gained Underwater Breathing, underwater mobility, flight, pressure resistance, and other enhancements, none of which were useful at that moment.

Ludwell suddenly opened his mouth and produced a silent screech. He then swept his hand toward the waves beside him, causing countless wind blades to freeze momentarily in midair.

Immediately following that, Admiral Hell raised his left hand as the black, square-shaped ring on his index finger produced a sinister and eerie glow that instantly brightened.

Whoosh!

The bronze door, which exuded an indescribable feeling, instantly swelled as it doubled in height and width.

Amidst a heavy creaking sound, the gap in the door opened up.

The already terrifying suction force immediately rose to an unimaginable level.

Blue wind blades and black sea waves swept Klein through the air toward the door, straight at the strange vines and arms extending outward.

Klein had planned to use the Priest of Light's Light of Holiness to clash head-on with the bronze door and gain a moment's respite, but then he saw a glaring white fireball half a person's height fly over.

The fireball's speed was augmented by the tremendous suction force generated by the door. It flew past Klein and smashed into the mysterious door's opening.

Boom!

The white flames scattered and rained down, but they only made the bronze door quake and dim slightly.

Klein took the opportunity and snapped his fingers.

The matchsticks he had separated in his pockets immediately ignited. Scarlet flames enveloped his body, and he melted away.

A flame burst out by the side of the bronze door as Klein leaped out of it.

He instantly noticed Anderson Hood floating rather awkwardly in midair with a burning-white spear in his palm.

The Strongest Hunter had finally arrived, though he appeared unaccustomed to flying.

When Ludwell looked up and saw this scene, the pale-white flames behind the silver mask clearly jumped twice.

Clearly, he had never expected the Future to have two other powerhouses at the pirate admiral level besides Admiral of Stars Cattleya. Furthermore, they wielded formidable mystical items and Sealed Artifacts.

At that moment, Ludwell suddenly raised a hand to his face and unexpectedly removed his silver mask.

Heavy, pale-white light suddenly spewed out from behind the mask, causing endless silence to instantly spread out from the black, square-shaped ring on Ludwell's left index finger.

The silence surged into the bronze door and lifted it off the deck and into the air.

The door covered in mysterious patterns fused with the endless silence as it rapidly burgeoned to more than thirty meters tall.

With the sea as its base, it stood there, erect like an entrance to another world, one completely different from the present world.

Creak!

The bronze door opened as unspeakable darkness surged out and enveloped the Black Tulip's bow.

Upon seeing this, Klein abandoned his attack, quickly took out the correct charm, and used it on himself.

A strong gust of wind swept over and lifted him above the Black Tulip.

Drawn by the darkness, the gigantic sailboat passed through the bronze door ten meters at a time, sailing into another world.

Admiral Hell Ludwell stood at the bow as he looked up into the sky. His face was blanketed by the pale-white luster, preventing anyone from discerning his looks.

His gaze first swept past Klein before landing on Anderson Hood. He seemed to make a mental note of these two hunters, but he didn't make any further attempts to attack. He seemed to have been restrained by the surrounding darkness.

Anderson was taken aback as he didn't hesitate to throw out the burning-white spear in his hand.

The spear shot straight at Ludwell, but once it entered the region enclosed by darkness and silence, it silently vanished.

Ludwell is planning to escape? How decisive... Klein was first stunned before he recalled that Azik's copper whistle was still on the Black Tulip.

With the gigantic sailboat halfway through the bronze door and about to enter another world, beyond his power to stop, Klein threw a matchstick and snapped his fingers.

He appeared fifty meters above the stern, where the undead creatures were fighting over Azik's copper whistle. The item, which kept changing hands, finally burst into scarlet flame due to the matchsticks tied to it.

Amidst the flames, Klein's figure surfaced there and grabbed Azik's copper whistle.

This was the preparation he had made to retrieve the copper whistle!

Furthermore, to prevent any mishaps, such as the undead creatures pulling away the matchsticks, he had also coated the copper whistle with a highly flammable essential oil of the Sun!

Pa!

Surrounded by countless undead, Klein didn't even have time to withdraw the hand holding the copper whistle. He immediately snapped his fingers again.

At this moment, transparent, rotting, pale, or illusory hands grabbed him!

The matchstick Klein had previously thrown into the air ignited in midair as it produced a flame.

His figure quickly materialized amid the flames. His face was ashen, and his lips were white.

After being grabbed by countless shadows, wraiths, and undead creatures, Klein felt an icy chill in the depths of his Spirit Body. Unable to control his body, he plummeted into the sea dyed gold.

With the Black Tulip almost completely crossing the bronze door, the sea which appeared like an entrance to hell had been restored to normal.

Klein sank several meters and swallowed a few mouthfuls of bitter, astringent seawater before finally recovering.

Thankfully, I was sufficiently prepared... As this thought flashed through his mind, he suddenly felt something amiss.

The Murloc Cufflink gave him the passive ability to breathe underwater for ten minutes. He shouldn't have swallowed mouthfuls of water!

Klein suddenly turned his head and looked at his wrist, only to realize that the blue cufflink had detached itself at some point in time.

It's lost... Tinder lost it... I was on the Black Tulip the entire time... Klein splashed a few times and floated to the surface, just in time to see the gigantic sailboat's tail meld into the darkness as the bronze door slowly closed.

He instinctively swam forward for a few seconds before finally stopping. He selected one of his remaining charms and bestowed the Beyonder effect of Underwater Breathing upon himself.

In midair, Anderson Hood clicked his tongue when he saw this.

"This fellow is really crazy...

"He's actually trying to engage in pursuit!"

At this moment, the starlight above the Future fell and condensed into a long bridge that extended over.

Cattleya had finally finished off the grayish-black monster with immense vitality!

What a pity. If only Admiral Hell had hesitated a little and hadn't fled so promptly... Anderson Hood sighed silently and landed firmly on the star bridge.

As he watched Gehrman Sparrow fly over, he was just about to greet and praise him when he saw the cold and gloomy expression.

Anderson instinctively made way and chuckled dryly, allowing Gehrman Sparrow to walk past him.

After returning to the Future via the star bridge, Klein restrained his surging emotions and watched Frank Lee walk over and give him a thumbs-up.

"You're the craziest fellow I've ever met!

"You actually dared to board the Black Tulip alone and single-handedly challenge Admiral Hell. You even returned alive!"

Sorry, in terms of craziness, I'm far inferior to you... Klein thought in response.

At this moment, pirates with long or disheveled hair expressed their astonishment and amazement.

In such an atmosphere, Klein closed his eyes and felt his Faceless potion completely digest.

Chapter 661: Approaching

Frank Lee sensed something amiss with Gehrman Sparrow and added with a chuckle, "I'd planned to help by tossing over a few seeds. Unfortunately, I can't throw that far."

Throw seeds at the Black Tulip? These waters contain the lingering aura of Earth Mother. Beyonder creatures and objects in the corresponding domain would mutate and attack everyone indiscriminately... And I was aboard the Black Tulip at the time... Thank goodness you didn't throw them... Klein suddenly recalled the Future's wretched state moments earlier and the watermelon that had sprouted from the dead pirate's head.

Just as he was about to give a curt answer befitting Gehrman Sparrow's persona, he saw Heath Doyle emerge from the shadows and double over to vomit.

The Bloodless retched, then his knees went limp and he collapsed onto the deck.

Ugh! Ugh!

He finally vomited a pool of yellowish-green liquid. Amid it lay a piece of half-rotten, grayish-black flesh that was still squirming slightly.

Ugh! Ugh! Ugh!

Heath Doyle continued vomiting similar items.

Despite his disgust, Klein felt greatly relieved at the sight. He had feared that Rose Bishop Heath Doyle had been corrupted by indiscriminately eating things, but it now seemed that Heath had isolated what he had consumed instead of truly digesting it.

As expected of a Rose Bishop who isn't mad... Klein silently sighed.

Just as he was planning to look away from the vomit, thoughts suddenly flashed in his mind.

Creeping Hunger has already been activated. It needs to be "fed" once within a day. And there aren't any outsiders here. There's also no ideal scum... The pirate who died won't do. Although his partners might not put too much weight on his corpse, Creeping Hunger devours the soul...

I wonder if these pieces of flesh can be treated as "food." At the very least, they contain tremendous vitality, having come from a corpse influenced by Earth Mother's aura...

With this in mind, Klein took two steps forward and came to Heath Doyle's side.

Unable to bear looking at the puddle of vomit, he instinctively turned toward the vast, sunlit sea beyond the gunwale.

Then, he reached out to a piece of grayish-black flesh with his left hand.

Creeping Hunger neither reacted nor split open a mouth.

Looks like it doesn't want to eat this... For now, I can only use it sparingly to deal with any latent dangers. If I still can't find suitable food by the end of the day, I'll throw it above the gray fog... Klein helplessly retracted his hand and looked up at the captain's cabin.

The golden brooch in front of Cattleya bloomed with light once again, materializing a Sun Wraith that cleansed the pieces of grayish-black flesh from Heath Doyle.

The pirate admiral's expression didn't seem to change. She merely looked a little tired, while the purple hue in her eyes grew increasingly obvious.

After confirming that the ship had set sail again, Klein returned to his room to change out of his drenched clothes.

Anderson glanced at him and curiously opened his mouth.

"Shut up!" Klein spoke out before he could.

The recent turn of events had cost him his Murloc Cufflink, so the man plagued by bad luck looked increasingly objectionable to him. Klein was a hair's breadth from classifying Anderson as food for Creeping Hunger.

"Alright." Anderson raised his arms. "I'll just drink in silence."

Klein ignored him and entered the cabin before returning to his room.

Inside the bathroom, he picked up a Water Creation charm and activated it in ancient Hermes. Once the tub was filled with clean water, he took off his clothes and slid in.

The ice-cold sensation and the warm sunshine significantly soothed him. He picked up a pen and paper he brought from his desk and wrote down a divination statement: "The location of the Murloc Cufflink."

After reciting it seven times, Klein reclined fully, using the edge of the bathtub as a pillow, and entered a dream.

Amidst a grayish blur and a disconnected illusory world, he saw a zombie rotting in several parts of the deck. The blue Murloc Cufflink was embedded in the flesh of the zombie's left waist.

Apart from the deck, it was pitch-black. It was impossible to tell where the ship was.

It really is on the Black Tulip... Klein opened his eyes and came to the conclusion.

I hope Admiral Hell wouldn't discover it. This way, I can use this cufflink to lock onto the Black Tulip's location...

It won't be a serious problem even if he discovers it. As long as Ludwell doesn't throw the cufflink away and too much time doesn't pass, I can use it to locate his ship. However, I'll have to perform the divination above the gray fog rather than in the real world.

Also, I need to disrupt divination later to prevent Admiral Hell from using the cufflink to lock onto me or even curse me.

That ring really does seem to be an item left behind by the ancient Death. Yes, I should write to Mr. Azik and tell him about it. Klein quickly rinsed himself and stepped out of the bathtub.

After drying himself, he changed into the Loen gentleman's suit he had worn before. He first reorganized his belongings and washed his clothes, then unfolded a sheet of paper and took out Azik's copper whistle.

Standing beside the desk and looking at the objects on it, Klein hesitated when he extended his right hand.

The light in his eyes flickered before he put away Azik's copper whistle and placed it into a small metallic box. He then isolated its aura with a wall of spirituality.

He planned on summoning the messenger only after he left these waters and the Future.

I suffered quite a huge loss this time. Thankfully, I finished digesting the Faceless potion. I can now just wait for the appearance of mermaids...

Yes... The actual state of this battlefield of the gods differs from what I imagined. It even bears the aura of Earth Mother...

It must've been left behind later; otherwise, it's impossible that a deity can't control "Her" own aura.

None of the eight ancient gods in the Second Epoch wielded the authority of the Earth domain...

There are several possibilities among "Their" subsidiary gods, such as the Giant Queen, Goddess of Harvest Omebella, or the Goddess of Life, who was subordinate to the Vampire Ancestor Lilith.

Was this a battle in which subsidiary gods participated, or did it not date from the Second Epoch at all? Klein knew too little about the battlefield of the gods, so he could only speculate and indulge in fanciful thoughts.

He focused again as he began cutting some paper figurines and drew The Fool's symbol of concealment and change on them.

Pa!

Klein raised the paper figurine and shook it.

Flames appeared out of thin air as the paper figurine burnt to a crisp.

By doing this, he could obtain the desired effects to a certain extent. If he wished for better effects, he had to go above the gray fog to respond. He could use the Black Emperor's card to stir the powers of the mysterious space, coupled with the paper angel to provide protection.

With Azik's copper whistle and Will Auceptin's paper crane interfering with a suspected existence's spying, Klein returned to the bathroom and methodically finished what he needed to do.

After tidying the room, he put on Creeping Hunger and Tinder, then slowly walked onto the deck, prepared to carefully observe his surroundings so he wouldn't miss any sign of mermaids.

Just after leaving the cabin, he saw Anderson Hood sitting against a wooden barrel of alcohol. His expression was gloomy and his aura subdued, as though he were lost in thought or melancholy.

He really kept his promise and has been drinking in silence? Klein muttered inwardly as he passed Anderson.

Anderson slowly looked up and asked as though he was in a reverie, "Is there a problem with the alcohol here?"

Klein was taken aback as he seriously replied, "Yes."

Anderson fell speechless.

This fellow is simply too unlucky. His Beyonder powers even failed to detect the problem with the alcohol? The corners of Klein's lips twitched as he continued forward.

Many sailors had gathered on deck, watching Nina assume the duties of a Lord of Storms priest and hold a wake for the recently deceased pirate.

After a simple prayer, Nina looked around and said, "Revere wished to be buried on the mountain in his hometown port after his death. The sunset there is the most beautiful.

"He wished to be cremated so that he wouldn't be disturbed after death.

"Holy Lord of Storms, bless him with eternal peace." Many of the sailors believed in the Lord of Storms as they struck their left breast with their right fists.

Klein didn't approach as he silently watched this scene from a distance.

After the wake was over, the corpse of the pirate, Revere, was reduced to ashes with the help of a scroll. Klein silently sighed as he inwardly drew a crimson moon.

For the rest of the day, the brilliant sunlight remained fixed at midday. The Future circled several ruins as it ventured deeper into those waters.

At some point in time, Anderson had recovered and arrived beside Klein.

He shot him a glance and pointed to the buildings that were submerged ahead.

"After passing these ruins, turn left and sail for about ten nautical miles. There should be a chance of encountering mermaids."

Finally... Klein was just about to answer when the sky suddenly darkened. The sunlight subsequently vanished.

Night had fallen again.

Without another word, he returned to his room and got into bed.

Soon, he found himself lucid within the dream. Clean floor-to-ceiling windows stood before him, along with neatly arranged tables and chairs and bookshelves filled with books.

He had returned to the spot from where he had previously left the dream. He was back in the library.

The glow of sunset streamed in, cloaking every object in a faint layer of gold. Puzzled, Klein walked to the bookshelf he had previously browsed.

Unsurprisingly, he saw Book of Charms and other books on mysticism.

Klein was about to take out the book and quickly read through it again when his gaze swept across the bookshelf opposite him. There, he saw a black-covered book titled Roselle's Notebook 3!

The emperor's diary? An entire diary? Klein subconsciously wanted to extend his hand.

At that moment, the mysterious eyes that had observed him and the deck flashed through his mind, along with the person who had opened the door in the hall of murals mentioned by Anderson Hood and the unnatural way he had been moved through the dream.

Klein retracted his gaze and took out the Book of Charms again.

He came to a long table, sat down, and began quickly browsing through it.

Suddenly, he heard footsteps approaching from the depths of the library.

Klein instantly tensed up as he slowly lifted his head.

The first thing that he saw was a pair of black leather boots.

Chapter 662: Powerful Aura

As his gaze moved up, Klein roughly guessed the owner of those footsteps.

She was a woman wearing beige trousers that allowed easy movement and a pair of tall black leather boots. Over the trousers, she wore a light brown skirt that reached her knees. Its layered hem fell diagonally, lending her a free-spirited, dashing air.

Her attire made Klein feel as though he had returned to Earth. No such style was fashionable in Loen, Intis, Feysac, Feynapotter, Lenburg, Masin, East Balam, or anywhere else!

Klein lifted his head faster and finally saw the lady's appearance.

Long chestnut hair cascaded naturally down her back. Her straight eyelashes curved outward just so, and her profound blue eyes seemed to conceal an ocean.

She was strikingly beautiful, but that was not what drew the eye most. Every movement naturally exuded nobility, leaving the powerful impression of someone long accustomed to an exalted position. Klein nearly lowered his head on instinct to avoid her gaze.

Furthermore, she's very tall. She's almost as tall as I am in my Klein Moretti form... Klein added inwardly.

After completely digesting the Faceless potion, he had realized that the self was an amalgamation of personality, experience, knowledge, and social connections. His looks and build could be changed at will; therefore, as long as he knew who he was, he didn't mind using the word "form" to describe the characteristics of each identity. After all, every Faceless transformation could be fixed in place without further maintenance. That also meant that even without the corresponding Beyonder powers, he could retain Gehrman Sparrow's appearance.

And because of that, he could easily make Klein Moretti grow taller if he wanted.

Tap. Tap. Tap. The lady approached Klein with the natural bearing of one accustomed to looking down from above. She pulled out a chair and sat.

"We meet again," the lady said in a gentle but emotionless tone.

The way she speaks sounds familiar... Klein's mind whirled as he remembered the source of the sense of familiarity.

The scene of her arrival echoed through his mind, and he finally focused on those black leather boots!

It's... It's her! Klein suddenly recalled the relevant scenes.

Back when he used his Spirit Body state to infiltrate the Royal Museum and retrieve the Black Emperor card, he had encountered a demigod. She had been sitting at the top of a wooden staircase in the middle of two large bookshelves. Her black leather boots back then were dangling in the air!

Later, after changing back into Sherlock Moriarty and calling for help while pursued by the Devil dog, he had encountered a forest path formed from green pea vines. He had no choice but to follow the carriage into midair, where he saw the pea vines interweave into a hammock beneath a pair of black leather boots!

It's her! Why would she appear in this dream world, in these waters? B-besides, she said, "we meet again." I'm Gehrman Sparrow right now! Amid these thoughts, Klein replied with a deadpan expression, "We haven't met before."

So what if you're a demigod? As long as you aren't an angel, I can use the Sea God Scepter to resist you in this dream world! Klein encouraged himself in secret.

The long-browed lady raised her chin and observed Klein for two seconds.

"Is that so? Mr. Hero Bandit Black Emperor..."

Klein's mind exploded with a boom, as though he had been struck by lightning. His thoughts shattered into countless fragments, leaving only chaos.

S-she knows that I'm Hero Bandit Black Emperor?

She recognized me as the Spirit Body who originally stole the Card of Blasphemy from the Royal Museum?

H-how is this possible!?

Wait, why did she address me directly as Hero Bandit Black Emperor? If she had called me Sherlock Moriarty, I would've been even more stunned and might have failed to hide my reaction...

As these thoughts flashed through his mind, Klein instinctively controlled his facial muscles and calmly said, "I don't know what you're talking about."

The unfashionably dressed lady neither repeated herself nor offered an explanation. Instead, she said directly, "I was the one who provided your identity as Gehrman Sparrow."

...

Klein instantly felt his scalp tingle. He felt as though he didn't have a single secret when facing her.

The identity of Gehrman Sparrow was created through Miss Sharron's connections... Is this demigod one of them?

Right, Miss Sharron had previously mentioned that someone in her circles had been investigating the true identity of Hero Bandit Black Emperor and had promised to fulfill any reasonable request in exchange. For ingredients, it was limited to those below that of High-Sequence Beyonder ingredients...

According to Miss Sharron's description, the person who offered the commission was over 1.7 meters tall, with a well-proportioned figure and long chestnut hair. She liked wearing black leather boots. Damn... isn't that the woman before me? Back then, I even suspected she was the demigod I met at the Royal Museum. Furthermore, she knew perfectly well that I had taken the Black Emperor card...

Thoughts flashed past as Klein was temporarily at a loss for words. All he could do was maintain his silence.

The lady, whose chin was slightly rounded rather than sharp, didn't dwell on Gehrman Sparrow's identity. She looked toward the frozen sunset and said, "While you were in the Rorsted Archipelago, Nast Solomon's Black Emperor also appeared in those waters.

"I believe you know what that means, Mr. Hero Bandit."

Realizing that she wasn't exposing his identity and was even offering clues and evidence, Klein could only twitch the corner of his lips and say, "The law of Beyonder characteristic convergence."

The expression of the lady opposite him immediately softened. Smiling, she said, "You really are Hero Bandit Black Emperor."

...So you weren't certain... You merely laid out the clues while projecting confidence... But how did she seem so certain earlier? Does she have other evidence she hasn't revealed? Klein felt a stab of regret and profound puzzlement.

The lady, who seemed to enjoy her status as an important figure, said nothing further. She looked at the bookshelf and asked, "You discovered through his diary that the bookmark contained the Black Emperor card?"

Diary... This lady also knows that the so-called Roselle's notebook is a diary... Klein was taken aback as he didn't reply to her.

"You didn't take that notebook because you sensed something?" the lady asked.

This... Klein suddenly realized something and decided to ask questions instead of answering. He wanted to extricate himself from this passive position rather than be led by the nose.

He looked at the lady's deep blue eyes and asked directly, "You are the mysterious person on the Future who was secretly spying on me?"

The beautiful but unapproachable lady replied frankly, "Yes. Cattleya doesn't know that I've secretly boarded the Future, yet you actually discovered me... A Clown's sense of danger?"

Based on the observation and the information from before, she can basically determine that I'm a Faceless, a Magician... From her tone, she's very familiar with Admiral of Stars... The upper echelons of the Moses Ascetic Order or that Queen Mystic? Klein nodded and said, "That's right."

The lady raised her chin, the corners of her lips curling slightly.

"It's impossible for a normal Clown to sense danger at this level, even if they're already a Sequence 5."

She has discovered another unique trait again... This... She knows the Seer pathway very well... Indeed, I've been somewhat affected by the gray fog's powers, giving me a direct premonitory intuition even in situations that aren't dangerous? Klein didn't give her a chance to speak again as he asked in a deep tone, "Were the sounds of the door opening and the footsteps heard by Anderson Hood in the depths of the hall of murals yours?"

"Are you referring to the hunter plagued by bad luck?" the lady asked thoughtfully.

"Yes." Klein nodded.

"It was indeed me." The lady paused. "Pass a prophecy on to that hunter. The deadliest danger often lies in everyday life."

What does that mean? Seeing that she had no intention of explaining, Klein deliberated before asking, "Were you the one who made me appear in the Saint of Darkness's dream?"

The lady ran her fingers through her long chestnut hair and said gently but coldly, "That's not hard to guess.

"Through that dream, I confirmed that you took the remains of that sea serpent, Kalvetua.

"And as before, you appeared in Spirit Body form. You took the item from Jahn Kottman and vanished immediately? Oh, you still carry the Black Emperor card."

Klein didn't respond to this topic and switched to asking, "You are Queen Mystic?"

"Many people call me by that name," the lady calmly answered.

It really is her... This lady's Loen bounty alone reaches 650,000 pounds! Klein silently drew a breath and said, "Ma'am, why are you looking for me?"

Queen Mystic used her blue eyes to look at him for a few seconds before saying, "I hope you can share with me the method to interpret the language created by Emperor Roselle. For that, I can satisfy most of your requests."

Klein neither admitted nor denied anything. He chuckled and asked, "Don't tell me you can even provide the corresponding High-Sequence Beyonder potion formulas?"

Queen Mystic subdued her bearing, but her dignity remained undiminished as she said, "Your fate lies on the main peak of the Hornacis Mountain Range."

This... Klein felt a baffling sense of shock.

His eyes darted around. While he was considering a question, Queen Mystic stood up.

"When you need some help or clues to a few Cards of Blasphemy, tell me the answer."

She turned around and walked into the depths of the tidy and vast library. With each step, her figure turned faint before quickly vanishing.

The library immediately collapsed. After a moment of feeling adrift, Klein found himself in the hall filled with beautiful murals.

Anderson looked at him in shock as he blurted out, "How did you get here?"

Chapter 663: False Alarm

After hearing Anderson's question, Klein was momentarily at a loss for an answer. He couldn't just tell him that he had fallen out of a dream suspected to belong to Queen Mystic.

He gave the unlucky Strongest Hunter a cold look, raised his right hand, and pointed upward.

"Is that so..." Anderson Hood nodded in enlightenment.

What... did you figure out? I don't even know what I was trying to express... The corner of Klein's lips twitched imperceptibly as he changed the subject. Recalling the encounter, he said, "I met someone just now..."

"Not someone we know? Not a member of the Future? The person who came out of the door from deep inside the hall?" Anderson suddenly became excited.

This fellow doesn't put on any airs as the Strongest Hunter. He's like a reporter chasing a scoop... Klein lampooned without answering him. He said directly, "She asked me to pass a prophecy on to you."

"She... What prophecy?" Anderson was somewhat puzzled.

If I were Klein Moretti, I would've answered, "Sorry, I forgot. She only said it once"... Imagining the prank he couldn't play, Klein said in a deep voice, "The deadliest danger often lies in everyday life."

Anderson listened carefully and drew a breath.

"That's far too accurate! I drank some beer and nearly turned myself into an idiot. Who would've imagined that most of the alcohol aboard the Future was problematic?"

He ruminated for a few seconds and asked, somewhat worried, "That's it?

"Did you forget any details or keywords?"

I can tell that you're being provocative... Klein ignored Anderson Hood and walked to the entrance of the hall of murals where he looked at Frank and Nina outside.

Back in the library, Queen Mystic had suddenly accused him of being Hero Bandit Black Emperor, inevitably placing him on the defensive. Afterward, he had tried to throw her off and avoid being led by the nose, so he remained extremely tense. His thoughts had been entirely focused on how to respond, leaving him no time to review the encounter and examine its details. Now, he finally had the chance.

First, the most important question: how much does Queen Mystic understand about my identity and Roselle's diary?

Yes... She believes I possess a method for interpreting the language Emperor Roselle created, not that I know the language itself. The distinction may be slight, but it is enough to prove that she hasn't discovered our identities as transmigrators or found any clues pointing to that conclusion.

Miss Sharron isn't a novice who just entered mysticism circles or became involved in complicated affairs. When asking someone to forge an identity for me, she couldn't possibly have divulged who it was for. Besides, the portrait I provided already showed Gehrman Sparrow...

That means Queen Mystic hasn't equated Sherlock Moriarty with Gehrman Sparrow. Yes, if she'd already known, as I previously imagined, a more effective form of address would've been Mr. Black Emperor Sherlock Moriarty. That would've struck my mental defenses several times harder than simply calling me Hero Bandit Black Emperor.

From her standpoint, how did she gather useful information from scattered clues?

She knows that the bookmark is the Black Emperor card. Hence, she connected the specter-like Hero Bandit Black Emperor with the strange Spirit Body who stole the Card of Blasphemy from the Royal Museum. Later, the King of the Five Seas, Nast, suddenly appeared in the Rorsted Archipelago. Based on the law of Beyonder characteristic convergence, she guessed that Hero Bandit Black Emperor might have appeared in Bayam and the archipelago.

During her investigation, she discovered the familiar identity of Gehrman Sparrow. His movements coincided with Hero Bandit Black Emperor's activities, so she formed a hypothesis and pursued it. She infiltrated the Future and observed me up close.

This is logical, but there's a certain level of coincidences in all of this. King of the Five Seas Nast appears anywhere he likes. Perhaps he might be missing a particular lady from the Red Theater and got his ship to cross the spirit world to enter the waters around the Rorsted Archipelago, or perhaps Bayam happens to have the High-Sequence Beyonder ingredients of the Black Emperor pathway that attracted him... There's no way to directly link it to Hero Bandit Black Emperor.

Of course, Queen Mystic making such a connection isn't incomprehensible. Perhaps, when it comes to important clues, she follows the principle that it's better to be wrong than sorry. Heh, that's a good habit, but an exhausting one.

There's also a high chance that she didn't lock onto Gehrman Sparrow immediately. However, with the reputation of the crazy adventurer Gehrman Sparrow becoming more prominent in the Rorsted Archipelago waters and the City of Generosity Bayam, she matched the timing after hearing the name, and then she came to a preliminary conclusion.

Sigh, it's still better to keep a low profile in life. Thankfully, my acting has finally come to an end. Subsequently, I can make the identity of Gehrman Sparrow vanish! Klein ran through everything and felt that he had mostly understood the problem at its root.

However, he had another thought and guess.

It was the way Admiral of Stars Cattleya had received Gehrman Sparrow on her ship. She had done it in an ostentatious manner, as though she was afraid that it wasn't known to the world that the crazy adventurer was cooperating with her!

Perhaps she was deliberately leaving a clue. Queen Mystic later arrived and learned that it was Gehrman Sparrow. After tracing my movements at sea, she formed a theory. Rather than bluffing, she might actually have been fairly certain! With one hand in his pocket, Klein left the hall of murals and headed straight for Cattleya outside the black cloister, intending to question her while her mind remained muddled.

At this point, he had actually relaxed significantly. This was because Queen Mystic clearly only knew that Gehrman Sparrow was equivalent to Hero Bandit Black Emperor, and he was a Beyonder who worked for some important figure. It didn't involve even more secrets.

Even if she had connected the loss of the Black Emperor card that night to Detective Sherlock Moriarty who was nearby, that wouldn't be a huge problem.

Long ago, I established that Sherlock Moriarty was The World and, in turn, a Blessed of The Fool. Gehrman Sparrow is merely an extension of that identity. Heh heh, I prepared precisely for a situation like this. I've always believed that I shouldn't treat others as fools. As long as someone remains active, traces of their movements and social connections will eventually expose them. That's why I prepared this identity in advance for clever people and strictly adhered to its premise even in daily life.

And it is entirely reasonable for the Blessed of an important figure to possess a basic understanding of Roselle's diary. That falls well within the bounds of an ordinary bestowment from a special existence.

Heh, you probably never expected that there's a mask under the mask!

Amid these thoughts, Klein also felt that the gray fog's help and his own caution had played significant roles. If he hadn't noticed the surveillance in advance and instinctively followed his intuition, he might have summoned the messenger under Queen Mystic's watch.

The messenger alone might not expose anything, but Queen Mystic might have a way to track it and find Mr. Azik. From his recent appearances, she might then uncover my identity as Klein Moretti... Klein crossed a square filled with numerous giant arrows, walked out of the black cloister, and saw Cattleya sitting there with her knees hugged to her chest, just as before. She was staring at the beautiful sunset.

Klein leaped onto the boulder and came to the side of the withered trees. He looked at the magnificent buildings on the opposite mountain and said in a seemingly normal manner, "At Nas, you showcased the star bridge to connect the dock to the ship. Were there any hidden motives behind that?"

Cattleya's head leaned to the side a little as she said, "I'm not telling you!"

Klein was momentarily at a loss.

He had imagined that the Admiral of Stars would be very forthcoming in the dream world. He had never expected this response. Of course, this too was a form of honesty, one true to her personality.

After two seconds of silence, Klein decided to pull a bluff.

"You wished to use this method to inform someone that I'm worthy of attention?"

Cattleya exhaled and remained sitting there, hugging her knees.

"Pretty much.

"It was mainly to tell others that if something serious happens or if I show obvious abnormalities, you will be the first clue."

As expected... Klein sighed inwardly.

He knew that the Admiral of Stars had resorted to such a petty trick to protect herself, but as The Fool, he needed to punish her for it.

However, I can't make an issue of this alone. It would make The Fool appear overly protective of "His" Blessed, which would be beneath "His" status... Yes, the Admiral of Stars must've done more than this. I can tally all these actions together and punish her for them... Klein quickly made up his mind. He was no longer as flustered by what he had encountered in this dream world.

As for Queen Mystic's suggestion, he didn't even consider it.

Emperor Roselle's diary contained matters of his transmigration and Earth. If he taught Queen Mystic how to read Chinese, she would discover this point and make even more critical guesses!

She must have reservations about the important figure who can casually lend out the Sea God Scepter, so she won't take forceful action. If I truly need her help, or if she gains leverage over me, I can agree to translate a few important pages of the diary that interest her. But I definitely won't teach her Chinese. Yes, even my translations will be diluted with synonyms. I only need to preserve the main points; that way, she can't reverse-engineer the language... Klein retracted his gaze and casually asked Cattleya, "Rumor has it that you and Queen Mystic had a falling-out, but that doesn't seem to be the case?"

Cattleya's glazed expression suddenly had a lively change. She pursed her lips and said, "What right do I have to fall out with her?

"I was just banished."

Banished... Klein was just about to ask when blinding sunlight beamed as he naturally woke up.

Looking at the bright sky outside, he wiped his forehead and muttered silently to himself.

What a terrifying dream.

After dealing with his wistfulness, Klein rolled off the bed and came to the deck. He continued his observation and awaited the appearance of mermaids.

Nearly an hour later, he finally heard a faint, indistinct voice coming from the distance.

Chapter 664: Every Second Counts

Mermaids? Klein felt delighted as anticipation and excitement filled him.

After nearly four months away from Backlund and a long succession of ordeals, he had finally reached his destination. He could now fulfill the last condition for advancing to Sequence 5: Marionettist!

Ever since entering these waters, the strain of waiting had steadily worsened his irritability and frustration. The absurd and amusing events aboard the Future, the incidents that became unusually terrifying upon reflection, and the dangers and unknowns hidden within the nights, noons, and dreams of the ruins of this war of gods had wound his nerves ever tighter. Every minute and every hour had been torment.

Now, the pressure from these emotions finally had a chance of being released!

Phew... Klein slowly heaved a sigh of relief. He directly returned to the cabin and entered the room that belonged to him.

Without panicking or letting joy go to his head, he followed his planned sequence and took out Azik's copper whistle and Will Auceptin's paper crane to interfere with any possible surveillance by Queen Mystic.

He took the remnant spirituality of ancient wraiths, a pair of eyes from a six-winged gargoyle, drago bark, and a metal bottle containing water from Sonia Island's Golden Spring out of his suitcase and arranged them across the desk. Then he entered the bathroom, locked the door, and expertly set up the bestowment ritual.

After setting up the ritual, he wasn't in a rush to go above the gray fog to respond to the prayer. Instead, he set up another ritual of summoning himself!

While taking four steps counterclockwise and chanting the incantation, Klein arrived above the gray fog. He responded to the summoning ritual and used his Spirit Body state to come to the real world. He then brought Tinder back to the mysterious space.

After doing all of that, Klein didn't relax. He came to the seat of The Fool, conjured a pen and paper, and quickly wrote the divination statement: "The singing ahead comes from mermaids."

Taking off his topaz pendant, he used divination to confirm the situation.

The singing ahead of the Future was from mermaids!

After calming his emotions, Klein summoned the iron cigar case from the junk pile and made it land on the ancient, mottled bronze table.

With a snapping sound, he opened the lid and saw the pupil-less All-Black Eye sitting there in silence. He could sense the extreme madness and danger from it, but it appeared to be in deep slumber.

After observing it for two seconds, Klein took out Tinder and slowly wore it on his right hand.

After completing all this, Klein reached out his right hand and spread his fingers without hesitation.

All kinds of motes of light surfaced before his eyes. Grayish-white, bronze-green, dark red, and black formed the foundation of this mysterious space.

Inside the All-Black Eye, an iron-black beam flared and thrashed amid the other colors.

Without using his spiritual intuition, Klein knew with certainty through his understanding of objects that the True Creator's mental corruption was represented by this iron-black light!

Feeling extremely wary, he closed his fingers and grabbed at the target before turning his wrist.

The iron-black light was instantly extracted and fused with Tinder. Immediately, the familiar illusory, evil, terrifying, and indescribable ravings boomed in his ears.

They shattered his train of thought and ground against his psyche, bringing a splitting pain to his brain, but the gray fog's power soon suppressed them into complete silence.

Klein didn't think further. Following the plan he had rehearsed countless times, he grabbed Tinder with his left hand, pulled it off, and threw it onto the stone floor of the magnificent palace.

Immediately afterward, Klein grabbed the All-Black Eye, which was now completely purified. He quickly responded to the bestowment ritual and passed the Marionettist Beyonder characteristic through the illusory door to the altar in the bathroom.

He didn't dare delay any further. He merely glanced at Tinder, which now exuded evil and was stained iron-black, its fingers twisting and its palm cracking open. Then he enveloped himself in spirituality and simulated a plummeting sensation to return to the real world.

Klein opened his eyes, grabbed the All-Black Eye from the altar, and ran into his room. As he did so, a thought occurred to him. If I let Creeping Hunger watch what happens to Tinder, I wonder what it would think...

Upon reaching the desk, Klein took out an iron pot that belonged to the Future and poured eighty milliliters of water from Sonia Island's Golden Spring into it.

As the pale gold liquid slowly spread out in a clear and transparent state, it made him subconsciously feel thirsty, as if he wished to drink a cup of it to quench his thirst.

Klein threw in the drago bark, the six-winged gargoyle's eyes, and the remnant spirituality of an ancient wraith, each producing a different reaction. Finally, the potion turned pale gold and looked ethereal, as though weightless.

At this critical juncture, Klein became abnormally calm instead. He steadily picked up the pupil-less black eye and dropped it into the potion.

He had already confirmed that the True Creator's mental corruption hadn't penetrated the gray fog or returned inside the All-Black Eye!

And this was something he had expected.

The All-Black Eye was instantly submerged by the pale gold liquid as the surface began bubbling.

Every bursting bubble made the potion turn blacker. About ten seconds later, all the changes came to a halt.

The potion in the pot had become a completely black liquid. It seemed to contain countless tiny, squirming worms invisible to the naked eye.

Klein took out a gold coin and made a quick divination as a confirmation.

After receiving a revelation confirming success, he heaved a sigh of relief, poured the Marionettist potion into a prepared metal bottle, and slipped it into his pocket.

He neither panicked nor rushed. Following his planned procedure, he quickly cleared the altar in the bathroom and retrieved Azik's copper whistle and Will Auceptin's paper crane.

At this point, he walked out of the cabin and onto the deck.

At that moment, the symbols and magical markings on the Future had lit up once again, forming a resplendent sea of stars that significantly muffled the mermaids' singing.

Rumors claimed that the singing of mermaids could make humans lose their reasoning and turn irrational. They would then jump off their ships to become food for the mermaids.

Klein subconsciously nodded at the window corresponding to the captain's cabin.

Admiral of Stars was standing there as her body swirled with starlight. The gaze she sent back at him appeared mixed.

Have you remembered what you said and how you acted in the dream? Klein lampooned. Maintaining a calm expression, he said, "I need a dinghy."

"It has already been prepared." Cattleya pointed toward the gunwale without any sign of surprise.

Gehrman Sparrow had mentioned that his purpose was to seek out mermaids back when he hired the Future!

Soon, Klein left the Future and the protection of the resplendent sea of stars. He headed into the ocean on his tiny dinghy.

As the singing grew louder, the voices seemed to burrow into his Spirit Body, numbing him and making him long to hear more.

This was far from sufficient for Klein. Besides, his spiritual intuition told him that he needed to be closer to make the singing clearer so as to satisfy the requirements of the ritual.

"Storm!"

Klein took out a charm made of tin, and he summoned a controllable gale that could push the dinghy ahead.

After an unknown period, the mermaids' singing grew louder. Their voices were so clear that they seemed to be singing softly beside Klein's ears. Every note stirred his Spirit Body, and the melody was intoxicating.

Klein felt his mind drift, and he nearly jumped into the sea and swam toward the source of the melodic singing.

He did his best to control himself and spotted numerous reefs ahead. Figures sat along their edges, singing.

These human-headed creatures possessed a certain beauty, with clear, innocent eyes. Their prominent breasts were covered in dark red scales, while the lower halves of their bodies consisted of massive fishtails that rhythmically struck the reefs.

The mermaids differed in appearance and scale color. From a human perspective, each possessed a distinct kind of beauty.

Klein relinquished his control of the dinghy, raised his right hand, and reached into his pocket for the potion.

At that moment, the mermaids sensed his approach and looked over.

Then, these creatures, who were also known as sirens, stopped singing out of shock. All of them leaped into the water with a splash.

Don't go... Klein feebly reached out with his right hand.

Isn't your singing supposed to lure humans here as food? Why are you fleeing when a human actually arrives? I'm not a bad person. I only came to hear you sing... At that moment, Klein's heart was filled with a resounding, What the fuck?

He soon discovered that the mermaids' singing hadn't stopped entirely. Farther along the reefs, a few mermaids had their backs to him. Because of the howling wind, they hadn't noticed their companions fleeing and continued singing without concern.

Klein's mind stirred as he thought for a moment and took out a charm.

This was the charm from the Sea God domain that gave the user affinity with underwater creatures!

"Storm!"

Amidst the incantation, blue flames enveloped the tin plate, making it vanish from the real world.

Although the remaining mermaids noticed him, they didn't flee in fear. Immediately, Klein took out the metal bottle containing the Marionettist potion and unscrewed the lid.

He made every second count so as to prevent any mishaps!

As he gulped down the somewhat bitter, musty potion, it flowed through his throat and into his stomach.

Suddenly, Klein found himself abnormally stiff. He felt as though he had returned to Tingen, back when he was being controlled by the strange puppet known as Sealed Artifact 2-049.

He tried moving his joints, but he felt as though they were filled with lead.

At the same time, he felt that tiny worms were boring into every one of his cells and into his Spirit Body.

His thoughts slowed as his mind registered the gradual loss of control over his body.

The mermaids' singing drifted over, stirring his desires and accumulated fanaticism and infatuation, allowing Klein to cling to the last remnants of his emotions. Through this temptation, he slowly escaped his petrified state.

A grayish-white fog quickly surfaced before his eyes as he heard the illusory ravings of "Hornacis... Flegrea... Hornacis... Flegrea..." Compared to his advancement to a Seer, Clown, and Magician, these ravings appeared staccato, as though they were being disrupted by something.

It's different from when I became a Faceless. The ravings are clearly much stronger. It can intermittently break through the obstacles produced from the fusion of the powers of the gray fog and reality... I can think again! Klein was delighted as he attempted to raise his arm.

His joints still felt heavy, but the feeling was weakening!

At the same time, Klein "saw" his present appearance.

His skin was yellowish-brown, like a puppet that had been buried alive for years with old bandages.

Fleshy tendrils were hidden beneath his skin as they squirmed, separated, and fused.

Klein immediately outlined the countless spherical lights in his mind, and he used Cogitation to calm his present state.

Throughout the process, the mermaids' singing continued to reverberate in his ears. His joints and muscles began to twitch, and the numbness slowly receded.

After an unknown period of time, Klein opened his eyes, his body having completely restored to normal.

He took a deep breath and silently sighed.

Finally...

I'm finally a Sequence 5!

I'm finally a Marionettist!

Chapter 665: Spirit Body Threads

Under the brilliant sunlight, the sea shone gold. Countless illusory black lines were reflected in the depths of Klein's eyes.

They extended from the nearby mermaids, from his own body, and from various points throughout the surrounding waters. Innumerable fine threads crowded his vision, each corresponding to a different place. Some stretched infinitely far, to the very edge of the void.

This strange, beautiful sight wasn't unfamiliar to Klein. On the few occasions when he had used the All-Black Eye's powers, including to create the fake World, he had seen similar scenes.

This was the source of a Marionettist's Beyonder powers!

According to the knowledge he had obtained from the potion, these illusory, thin black threads were known as Spirit Body Threads. By controlling them, one could directly influence a target's Soul Body, Astral Projection, Body of Heart and Mind, and Ether Body. Then, using the Ether Body as a bridge, one could control the target's physical body.

Thus, every Beyonder power of a Marionettist was founded upon Spirit Body Threads.

First, a Marionettist could locate hidden targets through the Spirit Body Threads present in every living creature. This was the best counter to a Demoness's invisibility and a Shadow Ascetic's concealment in shadows. Of course, Klein didn't know whether there were ways to hide one's Spirit Body Threads.

Second, a Marionettist could control a target like a puppet, slowing their thoughts and stiffening their body. This was a form of forceful control that was nearly impossible to defend against. Escaping it depended entirely on the strength of one's Spirit Body. A Sequence 5 Marionettist could control anyone below the demigod level with little difficulty.

Third, the control would deepen over time until the Marionettist had completely transformed the target into a marionette. Within a certain range, the Marionettist could remain behind the scenes and control the marionette in battle. The marionette could even use all its original Beyonder powers!

This is truly magical, terrifying, strange, and indescribable. It's the perfect thing for someone working behind the scenes. It's no wonder Rosago claimed that the control of Spirit Body Threads is one of the most difficult abilities to deal with for anyone that isn't a High-Sequence Beyonder...

But it requires time. Control cannot be instantly achieved. It requires a process that deepens with each step until it's ultimately achieved. To obtain initial control, the present me needs 20 seconds. But with the digestion of the potion, the time needed will clearly decrease. Once my Marionettist potion completely digests, I might be able to do it within five seconds. Yes, before the effects show, the target wouldn't even notice it. Perhaps those with prophetic abilities might be able to detect it...

Once initial control of a target is achieved, their thoughts will immediately slow, their movements will be hindered, and their body will stiffen. Then, bit by bit, they will turn into a puppet, a marionette. If a Marionettist has a partner, this process makes it easy to finish off a forcibly controlled target. Yes, if the enemy's Spirit Body isn't strong enough, the Marionettist will even have enough strength left to draw a gun or use a mystical item in concert with the control.

If the controlling process isn't disrupted by an external force, the target will become my marionette after five minutes. In a sense, the marionette is truly dead and the process is completely irreversible. Once the potion is completely digested, the time it takes to convert someone into a marionette will definitely be greatly shortened.

At present, I can control only one marionette. I can't be sure how many I'll control in the future, but the number will definitely increase. The apparent upper limit is three.

Not only does this require time, but it is also restricted by distance. I can only see Spirit Body Threads within a hundred meters, and I need to be within five meters of a target to control them...

When controlling a marionette, I cannot be more than a hundred meters away from it. I'm sure that range will increase in the future.

Heh, besides retaining its Beyonder powers, a marionette can use mystical items and Sealed Artifacts normally. Furthermore, it expends its own spirituality rather than mine. Of course, controlling the marionette still consumes my spirituality.

This ability suits me very well. In an extremely dangerous situation where I have no choice but to investigate, I can send the marionette in my place. Losing it would be painful, but that's better than dying myself. Heh heh, when Marionettist Rosago came to deal with me, he must've just completed another operation and lost his marionette in the process. Yes, he probably hadn't fully digested the Marionettist potion. That's why he couldn't establish initial control while waiting for me to open the door and had to come inside to converse with me.

In short, this truly lives up to a Sequence 5 Beyonder power! Furthermore, my previous Beyonder powers have also been enhanced by fifty percent or more...

I'm really looking forward to knowing what kind of qualitative changes I'll experience when I reach Sequence 4 when possessing so many strange powers. Sigh, I don't even know what its name is... Klein ended the inspection of his body and reflected wistfully.

Drawing on his experience setting activation and deactivation methods for Spirit Vision, he imposed a similar restriction on his ability to see Spirit Body Threads so he wouldn't see things he shouldn't.

Activating by tapping my left thumb twice on the first segment of my index finger. Repeating the action deactivates it. Same for the right hand... He reined in his thoughts and looked ahead, and he saw that the remaining mermaids had turned around and were looking at him with watery blue eyes.

At the thought that he would've lost control and become a Sealed Artifact akin to 2-049 without their singing or this simple ritual, Klein gave them a friendly smile.

Affected by the charm, the mermaids also moved their lips and showed a somewhat embarrassed smile.

As their pale purple or deep red lips parted, Klein saw rows of teeth as sharp as a wolf's fangs. They gleamed white, with viscous fluid dripping from them.

Klein was taken aback. He discovered that this was even more unacceptable for him than a real monster.

He had mentally prepared himself by thinking of mermaids as creatures that lived in the sea. Therefore, no matter how bizarre or terrifying they looked, he believed he could tolerate it. However, this exceeded his expectations. On the surface, mermaids were clearly beautiful and alluring women, and even their tails possessed an exotic beauty, yet their teeth were hideous and repulsive. Klein found the stark contrast almost unbearable and nearly looked away.

Waving his hand, he quickly took out a charm from the Sea God domain and created another gale of wind to push his dinghy back to the Future.

Along the way, Klein couldn't help but recollect the feelings he just had.

So the mermaids' singing is meant to neutralize and balance the potion's effects. Otherwise, most Faceless would be unable to withstand the Marionettist potion's corrosion of their Spirit Body Threads and would lose control immediately. Heh. Lucky exceptions like Kalvetua don't count. Perhaps its relative weakness was precisely due to the lack of a ritual.

In theory, any voice or Beyonder power with similar effects could replace a mermaid's song, but those who hadn't personally experienced it would be unable to distinguish the minute differences. Therefore, High-Sequence Beyonders outside the Seer pathway would find it difficult to offer effective guidance.

Amidst his thoughts, the dinghy returned to the Future. With a rope, Klein easily returned to the deck.

Anderson Hood stood by the gunwale and chuckled.

"So your search of mermaids was a requirement of a ritual, and not because you wish to obtain the corresponding ingredients."

"That's obvious," Klein succinctly replied.

Anderson shrugged and said, "No, it's not. A normal person will guess that mermaids are the ingredients you need, as this doesn't match the rituals needed by a Sequence 4. But who knew that Adventurer Gehrman Sparrow, who was at the level of a pirate admiral, was only a Sequence 6."

What do you mean "only?" Klein resisted the urge of controlling Anderson's Spirit Body Threads.

Ignoring the Strongest Hunter who automatically had a provocative halo, he entered the cabin and returned to his room.

Just as he approached the captain's cabin, he heard a creak as the door before him opened.

Cattleya didn't wear her heavy glasses. She looked at Gehrman Sparrow as the dark purple hue in her eyes swirled.

"Congratulations on your advancement.

"P-previously in the dream, did I say something?"

"You know what you said," Klein replied expressionlessly.

Cattleya fell silent for two seconds before saying, "You questioned me about my relationship with Queen Mystic?"

As she spoke, she couldn't help but look around.

She guessed that Queen Mystic might be on the Future because of my rather sudden question? I really have to be careful when conversing with a smart person... Klein nodded, walked past her and returned to his room, leaving the corridor in silence.

Only after he opened the door and stepped inside did he hear Cattleya's voice ring out from the captain's cabin and reverberate throughout the ship.

"Set sail for the return voyage."

Inside the room, Klein waited a few minutes before reentering the bathroom. He set up the ritual again and threw Creeping Hunger, which was turning more irascible due to the lack of food, above the gray fog.

After doing this, he wasn't in a rush to leave. He summoned the distorted iron-black Tinder and made it land before him.

After studying it for a moment, Klein placed Tinder inside the iron cigar case and threw it into the junk pile.

Although this expensive glove was now unusable, Klein was certain even trash could still prove useful.

Immediately following that, he slowly took off the already silent Creeping Hunger, and he similarly placed it into the junk pile.

Phew... Klein remained silent for two seconds before leaving the palace that looked like a giant's residence, and then he ventured deep into the mysterious space above the gray fog.

From his previous exploration, he discovered a bright staircase that seemed to lead to heaven. He suspected that the number of steps had something to do with his Sequence; therefore, he immediately came here to confirm it after he advanced.

After walking for a while, Klein finally saw the sacred staircase made of pure light. It had gained another step, bringing the total to five.

Indeed... Klein sighed, unsurprised.

Then, he climbed up the staircase that appeared to be prepared for giants and arrived at the top.
